# Coverage matrix

One row per datasheet (Rev. 7) parameter: register, bit field, interrupt source, state, electrical or
timing parameter, pin, OTP value. Each row names the driver API, the model behaviour and the test suite
with its files; the status says whether the behaviour is covered. The source is
[`coverage-matrix.csv`](coverage-matrix.csv); this page is generated from it by
`python3 tools/coverage_matrix.py write`, and `python3 tools/coverage_matrix.py check` verifies the totals
against [Coverage](coverage.md).

**Totals: 1090 rows; OK 729, INFO 360, TODO 1.**

The test suite ids (`T01` to `T28`) name groups of tests; the files column lists where they live. A suite
is not one test case: it is the set of cases in those files that exercise the behaviour.

| Suite | Test cases | Files |
| --- | --- | --- |
| T01 | every I/O call returns NotInitialized before EnsureInitialized | tests/test_config.cpp; inc/pf1550_codec.hpp (static_assert) |
| T02 | EnsureInitialized reads DEVICE_ID and the charger, writes nothing; Config without Limits is rejected before any I/O; failures of the bus and of identity |  |
| T03 | Limits are mandatory and must be sane; charger values are on the grid and inside Limits | tests/test_configure.cpp |
| T04 | every safety downgrade needs its acknowledgement; rails: windows, grids, DVS declaration, low power | tests/test_model.cpp; tests/test_configure.cpp |
| T05 | coin cell, power block and power-down order need their acknowledgements |  |
| T06 | an enumerator that is not one is rejected; codecs: grids, anchors and reserved codes |  |
| T07 | charger block writes the documented bytes and leaves the mode last; charger mode LinearOnly leaves charging off; reserved bits and fields the block does not own are preserved; JEITA, DPM, debounce and interrupt options reach their fields | tests/test_charger.cpp |
| T08 | the BATFET fault policy and DISBATFET are written when acknowledged | tests/test_charger.cpp |
| T09 | buck and LDO blocks: enables, voltages, current limit, forced PWM; the rail order is the caller's; a DVS step waits for the done flag, and an unchanged voltage does not; a DVS step that never finishes times out | tests/test_charger.cpp |
| T10 | coin cell, USB PHY, LED and power control reach their registers; reset paths and UVDET are written only when asked and acknowledged | tests/test_configure.cpp; tests/test_charger.cpp |
| T11 | Configure refuses charging while the charger reports a fault | tests/test_charger.cpp |
| T12 | a write that does not hold is reported; a bus error in the middle of Configure stops it | tests/test_irq.cpp |
| T13 | no write reaches a register that is not meant to be written | tests/test_power.cpp |
| T14 | the interrupt mask block writes defined bits and preserves reserved ones | tests/test_rails.cpp; tests/test_configure.cpp |
| T15 | charger states decode from CHG_SNS, including reserved codes; battery states, flags and the watchdog readings; VBUS flags decode | tests/test_irq.cpp; tests/test_rails.cpp |
| T16 | a charger state that changes between the two reads is flagged | tests/test_power.cpp; tests/test_rails.cpp; tests/test_review.cpp |
| T17 | charging is enabled only with limits-covered registers and no fault; charging with unsafe registers is refused whatever Configure did; SetChargerMode(Charge) checks the as-found values and reports them | tests/test_configure.cpp |
| T18 | RestartCharger cycles off and on, a bounded number of times | tests/test_power.cpp; tests/test_configure.cpp |
| T19 | the watchdog is serviced by WDTCLR = 1 and the other bits are kept; as found: option A1 starts the charger with no fast-charge timer; as found: MakeSafe writes CHG_OPER = linear only and verifies; as found: acknowledgements make an untimed charger acceptable; as found: limits below the OTP charge values give OutOfLimits; as found: a charger that is off is not checked; as found: each OTP option reads back its identity | tests/test_lifecycle.cpp; tests/test_model.cpp |
| T20 | ReadRail decodes each option's start-up values; load switch and low power flags read back |  |
| T21 | SetRailVoltage: LDOs, DVS bucks and the refusals; a rail with no window is never driven | tests/test_lifecycle.cpp; tests/test_errors.cpp |
| T22 | variants: A0 and unlisted flavour bytes are reported, never an error; SILICON_REV and the address code are reported raw; power states decode and a reserved code is Unknown; SetRailModes writes the four enables and checks the low power rule | tests/test_irq.cpp |
| T23 | the load switch is one way and needs a window and a token |  |
| T27 | the STANDBY pin goes through the port, or the call is Unsupported |  |

| PMS-SLEEP/LPSR | PMIC state SLEEP/LPSR (STATE_INFO 001110) | 10.2 | ReadPowerState() decodes STATE_INFO; other codes -> Unknown | model state machine | T13: tests/test_power.cpp | OK |
| PMS-REGS_DISABLE | PMIC state REGS_DISABLE (STATE_INFO 101011; home state) | Table 83 | ReadPowerState() decodes STATE_INFO; other codes -> Unknown | model state machine | T13: tests/test_power.cpp | OK |
| PMS-CORE_OFF | PMIC state CORE_OFF (no STATE_INFO code (system off, no I2C) (C36)) | Table 83 | not readable; EnterShip/EnterCoreOff reach it | model state machine | T16: tests/test_power.cpp; tests/test_rails.cpp; tests/test_review.cpp | OK |
